\n60\t            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n61\t                continue;\n62\t            }\n63\t            \n64\t            points[count] = {x, y};\n65\t       if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added\n134\t        \n135\t        in_cluster[closest] = true;\n136\t        members.push_back(closest);\n137\t    }\n138\t    \n139\t    // Copy QT Clustering Benchmark - Simplified Sequential Version\n// \n// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters\n// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point\n// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n    \n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n    \n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n        \n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n        \n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n            \n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            \n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n// Returns -1 if members\n        double max_dist = 0.0;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = cluster_members[i];\n            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);\n            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);\n        }\n        \n        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best\n        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {\n            min_diameter = max_dist;\n            closest_point = candidate;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return closest_point;\n}\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                              const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                              const double threshold,\n                              const int point_count,\n                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {\n    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    \n    // }\n    \n    // Copy members if requested\n    if (cluster_members) {\n        *cluster_members = members;\n    }\n    \n    return static_cast<int>(members.size());\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering       unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n    \n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        int best_seed = -1;\n        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;\n        \n        // Try each unclustered point as a seed\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {\n            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\n            if (clustered[seed]) continue;\n            \n            std::vector<int> candidate_members;\n            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, \n                                                       threshold, N, \n                                                       &candidate_members);\n            \n            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {\n                max_cardinality = cardinality;\n                best_seed = seed;\n                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;\n            }\n        }\n        \n        // If we found a cluster, add it\n        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\n            Cluster cluster;\n            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;\n            clusters.push_back(cluster);\n            \n            // Mark all members as clustered\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;\n            }\n            \n            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-fable-5","id":"msg_011Cf1SunxxxJiMHQSsk6Vfr","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01SuPH2RBw5u2EJRm5YpAciG","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/home/llmtest/evals/20260901-162328/qtclustering_claude-fable-5-cc-medium_cuda_r5/qtclustering/qtclustering.cpp","content":"// QT Clustering Benchmark - CUDA Parallel Version\n//\n// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters\n// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point\n// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.\n//\n// Parallelization: in each round of the outer loop, a candidate cluster is\n// grown from every unclustered seed point in parallel. A pool of persistent\n// worker blocks strides over the seeds; each block grows one candidate\n// cluster at a time, with its threads cooperatively scanning candidates.\n// Each worker maintains an incremental per-candidate array\n//   maxdist[i] = max distance from point i to the current cluster members,\n// so adding a member costs O(N) instead of O(|cluster| * N). Since max is\n// order-independent this is numerically identical to recomputing from\n// scratch, and argmin ties are broken by the lowest point index, exactly\n// matching the sequential algorithm.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                      \\\n    do {                                                                      \\\n        cudaError_t err__ = (call);                                           \\\n        if (err__ != cudaSuccess) {                                           \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d: %s\\n\", #call, __FILE__,  \\\n                    __LINE__, cudaGetErrorString(err__));                     \\\n            exit(1);                                                          \\\n        }                                                                     \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n\n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n\n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n\n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n\n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n\n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// CUDA kernels\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n#define BLOCK_SIZE 256\n\n// Block-wide argmin over (value, index) pairs; ties resolved to the lowest\n// index, matching the sequential scan order. Result left in s_val[0]/s_idx[0].\n__device__ inline void blockArgmin(double* s_val, int* s_idx) {\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    for (int stride = BLOCK_SIZE / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {\n        if (tid < stride) {\n            const double ov = s_val[tid + stride];\n            const int oi = s_idx[tid + stride];\n            if (ov < s_val[tid] || (ov == s_val[tid] && oi >= 0 && oi < s_idx[tid])) {\n                s_val[tid] = ov;\n                s_idx[tid] = oi;\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n}\n\n// Grow a candidate cluster from seed `seed` inside a single block.\n// maxdist[] is scratch of length N. If record != nullptr, the chosen members\n// are flagged in record[] and also marked in clustered[]. Returns (in\n// s_result) the cardinality of the grown cluster.\n__device__ int growCluster(const double2* __restrict__ points,\n                           const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,\n                           double* __restrict__ maxdist,\n                           const int seed, const int N, const double threshold,\n                           unsigned char* record) {\n    __shared__ double s_val[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ int s_idx[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ int s_next;\n\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    const double INF = std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity();\n\n    // maxdist == INF marks points that can never join this cluster:\n    // already globally clustered, already a member, or over threshold.\n    for (int i = tid; i < N; i += BLOCK_SIZE) {\n        maxdist[i] = (i == seed || clustered[i]) ? INF : 0.0;\n        if (record) record[i] = (i == seed) ? 1 : 0;\n    }\n    if (record && tid == 0) {\n        const_cast<unsigned char*>(clustered)[seed] = 1;\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    int members = 1;\n    int newpt = seed;\n\n    while (members < N) {\n        const double2 np = points[newpt];\n        double best_val = INF;\n        int best_idx = -1;\n\n        // Fused pass: fold the newly added member into maxdist[] and find\n        // the eligible candidate with the smallest resulting diameter.\n        for (int i = tid; i < N; i += BLOCK_SIZE) {\n            double m = maxdist[i];\n            if (m < threshold) {\n                const double2 p = points[i];\n                const double dx = p.x - np.x;\n                const double dy = p.y - np.y;\n                const double d = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n                if (d > m) m = d;\n                if (m < threshold) {\n                    maxdist[i] = m;\n                    if (m < best_val) {\n                        best_val = m;\n                        best_idx = i;\n                    }\n                } else {\n                    maxdist[i] = INF; // permanently ineligible (max only grows)\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        s_val[tid] = best_val;\n        s_idx[tid] = best_idx;\n        __syncthreads();\n        blockArgmin(s_val, s_idx);\n\n        if (tid == 0) {\n            s_next = s_idx[0];\n            if (s_next >= 0) {\n                maxdist[s_next] = INF;\n                if (record) {\n                    record[s_next] = 1;\n                    const_cast<unsigned char*>(clustered)[s_next] = 1;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        if (s_next < 0) break; // no more points can be added\n        newpt = s_next;\n        members++;\n    }\n\n    return members;\n}\n\n// Phase 1: every worker block grows candidate clusters for a strided subset\n// of the unclustered seeds and records each cluster's cardinality.\n__global__ void candidateKernel(const double2* __restrict__ points,\n                                const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,\n                                const int* __restrict__ seeds,\n                                const int num_seeds, const int N,\n                                const double threshold,\n                                double* __restrict__ maxdist_pool,\n                                int* __restrict__ cardinality) {\n    double* maxdist = maxdist_pool + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * N;\n    for (int s = blockIdx.x; s < num_seeds; s += gridDim.x) {\n        const int card = growCluster(points, clustered, maxdist, seeds[s], N,\n                                     threshold, nullptr);\n        if (threadIdx.x == 0) cardinality[s] = card;\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n}\n\n// Phase 2: re-grow the winning cluster with a single block, flag its members\n// in `record` and mark them globally clustered.\n__global__ void growBestKernel(const double2* __restrict__ points,\n                               unsigned char* clustered,\n                               double* __restrict__ maxdist,\n                               const int seed, const int N,\n                               const double threshold,\n                               unsigned char* record) {\n    growCluster(points, clustered, maxdist, seed, N, threshold, record);\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm (GPU)\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    if (N == 0) return clusters;\n\n    // Device buffers\n    double2* d_points = nullptr;\n    unsigned char* d_clustered = nullptr;\n    unsigned char* d_record = nullptr;\n    int* d_seeds = nullptr;\n    int* d_card = nullptr;\n    double* d_maxdist_pool = nullptr;\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_points, sizeof(double2) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_clustered, N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_record, N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_seeds, sizeof(int) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_card, sizeof(int) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_points, points.data(), sizeof(double2) * N,\n                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(d_clustered, 0, N));\n\n    // Size the persistent worker pool: enough blocks to saturate the GPU,\n    // capped by available memory for the per-worker maxdist scratch.\n    int device = 0;\n    cudaDeviceProp prop;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDevice(&device));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&prop, device));\n    int workers = prop.multiProcessorCount * 16;\n    size_t free_mem = 0, total_mem = 0;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemGetInfo(&free_mem, &total_mem));\n    const size_t per_worker = sizeof(double) * static_cast<size_t>(N);\n    const size_t mem_cap = static_cast<size_t>(free_mem * 0.9) / per_worker;\n    if (mem_cap < static_cast<size_t>(workers))\n        workers = std::max(1, static_cast<int>(mem_cap));\n    workers = std::min(workers, N);\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_maxdist_pool,\n                          per_worker * static_cast<size_t>(workers)));\n\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices(N);\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) unclustered_indices[i] = i;\n\n    std::vector<int> cardinality(N);\n    std::vector<unsigned char> record(N);\n\n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        const int num_seeds = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_seeds, unclustered_indices.data(),\n                              sizeof(int) * num_seeds, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n        const int grid = std::min(workers, num_seeds);\n        candidateKernel<<<grid, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_points, d_clustered, CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(record.data(), d_record, N,\n                                  cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n\n            Cluster cluster;\n              std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),\n CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_card));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_seeds));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_record));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_clustered));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_points));\n\n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>&                    points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\",\n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n\n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\",\n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];\n            if (membership[member] >= 0) {\n                printf(\"ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\\n\",\n                       member, membership[member], c);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Count clustered points\n    int clustered_count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {\n        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\\n\",\n           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);\n\n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int num_points = 1000;\n    double threshold A pool of persistent\n// worker blocks strides over the seeds; each block grows one candidate\n// cluster at a time, with its threads cooperatively scanning candidates.\n// Each worker maintains an incremental per-candidate array\n//   maxdist[i] = max distance from point i to the current cluster members,\n// so adding a member costs O(N) instead of O(|cluster| * N). Since max is\n// order-independent this is numerically identical to recomputing from\n// scratch, and argmin ties are broken by the lowest point index, exactly\n// matching the sequential algorithm.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                      \\\n    do {                                                                      \\\n        cudaError_t err__ = (call);                                           \\\n        if (err__ != cudaSuccess) {                                           \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d: %s\\n\", #call, __FILE__,  \\\n                    __LINE__, cudaGetErrorString(err__));                     \\\n            exit(1);                                                          \\\n        }                                                                     \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n\n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n\n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n\n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n\n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n\n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// CUDA kernels\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n#define BLOCK_SIZE 256\n\n// Block-wide argmin over (value, index) pairs; ties resolved to the lowest\n// index, matching the sequential scan order. Result left in s_val[0]/s_idx[0].\n__device__ inline void blockArgmin(double* s_val, int* s_idx) {\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    for (int stride = BLOCK_SIZE / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {\n        if (tid < stride) {\n            const double ov = s_val[tid + stride];\n            const int oi = s_idx[tid + stride];\n            if (ov < s_val[tid] || (ov == s_val[tid] && oi >= 0 && oi < s_idx[tid])) {\n                s_val[tid] = ov;\n                s_idx[tid] = oi;\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n}\n\n// Grow a candidate cluster from seed `seed` inside a single block.\n// maxdist[] is scratch of length N. If record != nullptr, the chosen members\n// are flagged in record[] and also marked in clustered[]. Returns (in\n// s_result) the cardinality of the grown cluster.\n__device__ int growCluster(const double2* __restrict__ points,\n                           const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,\n                           double* __restrict__ maxdist,\n                           const int seed, const int N, const double threshold,\n                           unsigned char* record) {\n    __shared__ double s_val[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ int s_idx[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ int s_next;\n\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    const double INF = std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity();\n\n    // maxdist == INF marks points that can never join this cluster:\n    // already globally clustered, already a member, or over threshold.\n    for (int i = tid; i < N; i += BLOCK_SIZE) {\n        maxdist[i] = (i == seed || clustered[i]) ? INF : 0.0;\n        if (record) record[i] = (i == seed) ? 1 : 0;\n    }\n    if (record && tid == 0) {\n        const_cast<unsigned char*>(clustered)[seed] = 1;\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    int members = 1;\n    int newpt = seed;\n\n    while (members < N) {\n        const double2 np = points[newpt];\n        double best_val = INF;\n        int best_idx = -1;\n\n        // Fused pass: fold the newly added member into maxdist[] and find\n        // the eligible candidate with the smallest resulting diameter.\n        for (int i = tid; i < N; i += BLOCK_SIZE) {\n            double m = maxdist[i];\n            if (m < threshold) {\n                const double2 p = points[i];\n                const double dx = p.x - np.x;\n                const double dy = p.y - np.y;\n                const double d = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n                if (d > m) m = d;\n                if (m < threshold) {\n                    maxdist[i] = m;\n                    if (m < best_val) {\n                        best_val = m;\n                        best_idx = i;\n                    }\n                } else {\n                    maxdist[i] = INF; // permanently ineligible (max only grows)\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        s_val[tid] = best_val;\n        s_idx[tid] = best_idx;\n        __syncthreads();\n        blockArgmin(s_val, s_idx);\n\n        if (tid == 0) {\n            s_next = s_idx[0];\n            if (s_next >= 0) {\n                maxdist[s_next] = INF;\n                if (record) {\n                    record[s_next] = 1;\n                    const_cast<unsigned char*>(clustered)[s_next] = 1;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        if (s_next < 0) break; // no more points can be added\n        newpt = s_next;\n        members++;\n    }\n\n    return members;\n}\n\n// Phase 1: every worker block grows candidate clusters for a strided subset\n// of the unclustered seeds and records each cluster's cardinality.\n__global__ void candidateKernel(const double2* __restrict__ points,\n                                const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,\n                                const int* __restrict__ seeds,\n                                const int num_seeds, const int N,\n                                const double threshold,\n                                double* __restrict__ maxdist_pool,\n                                int* __restrict__ cardinality) {\n    double* maxdist = maxdist_pool + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * N;\n    for (int s = blockIdx.x; s < num_seeds; s += gridDim.x) {\n        const int card = growCluster(points, clustered, maxdist, seeds[s], N,\n                                     threshold, nullptr);\n        if (threadIdx.x == 0) cardinality[s] = card;\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n}\n\n// Phase 2: re-grow the winning cluster with a single block, flag its members\n// in `record` and mark them globally clustered.\n__global__ void growBestKernel(const double2* __restrict__ points,\n                               unsigned char* clustered,\n                               double* __restrict__ maxdist,\n                               const int seed, const int N,\n                               const double threshold,\n                               unsigned char* record) {\n    growCluster(points, clustered, maxdist, seed, N, threshold, record);\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm (GPU)\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    if (N == 0) return clusters;\n\n    // Device buffers\n    double2* d_points = nullptr;\n    unsigned char* d_clustered = nullptr;\n    unsigned char* d_record = nullptr;\n    int* d_seeds = nullptr;\n    int* d_card = nullptr;\n    double* d_maxdist_pool = nullptr;\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_points, sizeof(double2) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_clustered, N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_record, N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_seeds, sizeof(int) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_card, sizeof(int) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_points, points.data(), sizeof(double2) * N,\n                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(d_clustered, 0, N));\n\n    // Size the persistent worker pool: enough blocks to saturate the GPU,\n    // capped by available memory for the per-worker maxdist scratch.\n    int device = 0;\n    cudaDeviceProp prop;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDevice(&device));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&prop, device));\n    int workers = prop.multiProcessorCount * 16;\n    size_t 0;"," }"]}],"originalFile":"// QT Clustering Benchmark - Simplified Sequential Version\n// \n// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters\n// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point\n// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n    \n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n    \n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n        \n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n        \n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n            \n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            \n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n// Returns -1 if no such point exists\nint findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& in_cluster,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold,\n                     const int point_count) {\n    int closest_point = -1;\n    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n    \n    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate\n    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {\n        // Skip if already clustered or already in this cluster\n        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;\n        \n        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members\n        double max_dist = 0.0;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = cluster_members[i];\n            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);\n            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);\n        }\n        \n        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best\n        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {\n            min_diameter = max_dist;\n            closest_point = candidate;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return closest_point;\n}\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                              const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                              const double threshold,\n                              const int point_count,\n                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {\n    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    \n    // Add seed point\n    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;\n    members.push_back(seed_point);\n    \n    // Iteratively add closest points\n    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {\n        // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, \n                                             threshold, point_count);\n        \n        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added\n        \n        in_cluster[closest] = true;\n        members.push_back(closest);\n    }\n    \n    // Copy members if requested\n    if (cluster_members) {\n        *cluster_members = members;\n    }\n    \n    return static_cast<int>(members.size());\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    \n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n    \n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        int best_seed = -1;\n        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;\n        \n        // Try each unclustered point as a seed\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {\n            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\n            if (clustered[seed]) continue;\n            \n            std::vector<int> candidate_members;\n            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, \n                                                       threshold, N, \n                                                       &candidate_members);\n            \n            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {\n                max_cardinality = cardinality;\n                best_seed = seed;\n                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;\n            }\n        }\n        \n        // If we found a cluster, add it\n        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\n            Cluster cluster;\n            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;\n            clusters.push_back(cluster);\n            \n            // Mark all members as clustered\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;\n            }\n            \n            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n            unclustered_indices.erase(\n                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),\n                unclustered_indices.end()\n            );\n        } else {\n            // No more clusters can be formed\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n    \n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n    \n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\n        \n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], \n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n        \n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\", \n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n        \n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\", \n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];\n            if (membership[member] >= 0) {\n                printf(\"ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\\n\",\n                       member, membership[member], c);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Count clustered points\n    int clustered_count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {\n        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\\n\",\n           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);\n    \n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int num_points = 1000;\n    double threshold = 2.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            num_points = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-t\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            threshold = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    if (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0) {\n        printf(\"Error: Invalid parameters (num_points=%d, threshold=%.2f)\\n\", \n               num_points, threshold);\n        return 1;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"QT Clustering Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Number of points: %d\\n\", num_points);\n    printf(\"Distance threshold: %.2f\\n\", threshold);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    \n    // Generate synthetic data\n    std::vector<Point> points(num_points);\n    generateSyntheticData(points, num_points);\n    \n    // Perform QT clustering\n    auto cluster_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    \n    const std::vector<Cluster> clusters = qtClustering(points, threshold);\n    \n    auto cluster_end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto cluster_time = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(\n        cluster_end - cluster_start);\n    \n    printf(\"Clustering time: %ld ms\\n\", cluster_time.count());\n    printf(\"Clusters found: %zu\\n\", clusters.size());\n    \n    // Calculate statistics and performance metrics\n    int total_clustered = 0;\n    int max_cluster_size = 0;\n    \n    for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters.size(); ++i) {\n        const int size = static_cast<int>(clusters[i].members.size());\n        total_clustered += size;\n        max_cluster_size = std::max(max_cluster_size, size);\n    }\n    \n    const double avg_cluster_size = clusters.empty() ? 0.0 : \n        static_cast<double>(total_clustered) / clusters.size();\n    \n    printf(\"Points clustered: %d / %d (%.1f%%)\\n\", \n           total_clustered, num_points, \n           100.0 * total_clustered / num_points);\n    printf(\"Average cluster size: %.2f\\n\", avg_cluster_size);\n    printf(\"Maximum cluster size: %d\\n\", max_cluster_size);\n    \n    // Performance metrics\n    const double time_sec = cluster_time.count() / 1000.0;\n    const double clusters_per_sec = clusters.size() / time_sec;\n    const double points_per_sec = num_points / time_sec;\n    printf(\"Performance: %.1f clusters/s, %.1f points/s\\n\", \n           clusters_per_sec, points_per_sec);\n    \n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        // Serialize cluster membership for hashing\n        std::vector<double> membershipData;\n        membershipData.reserve(num_points);\n        std::vector<int> membership(num_points, -1);\n        for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n                membership[clusters[c].members[i]] = static_cast<int>(c);\n            }\n        }\n        for (int m : membership) {\n            membershipData.push_back(static_cast<double>(m));\n        }\n        print_results(membershipData, \"ClusterMembership\");\n    }\n    \n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        const bool valid = validateClusters(clusters, points, threshold);\n        \n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return 0;\n}\n","userModified":false}}

A pool of persistent\n// worker blocks strides over the seeds; each block grows one candidate\n// cluster at a time, with its threads cooperatively scanning candidates.\n// Each worker maintains an incremental per-candidate array\n//   maxdist[i] = max distance from point i to the current cluster members,\n// so adding a member costs O(N) instead of O(|cluster| * N). Since max is\n// order-independent this is numerically identical to recomputing from\n// scratch, and argmin ties are broken by the lowest point index, exactly\n// matching the sequential algorithm.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                      \\\n    do {                                                                      \\\n        cudaError_t err__ = (call);                                           \\\n        if (err__ != cudaSuccess) {                                           \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d: %s\\n\", #call, __FILE__,  \\\n                    __LINE__, cudaGetErrorString(err__));                     \\\n            exit(1);                                                          \\\n        }                                                                     \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n\n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n\n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n\n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n\n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n\n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// CUDA kernels\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n#define BLOCK_SIZE 256\n\n// Block-wide argmin over (value, index) pairs; ties resolved to the lowest\n// index, matching the sequential scan order. Result left in s_val[0]/s_idx[0].\n__device__ inline void blockArgmin(double* s_val, int* s_idx) {\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    for (int stride = BLOCK_SIZE / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {\n        if (tid < stride) {\n            const double ov = s_val[tid + stride];\n            const int oi = s_idx[tid + stride];\n            if (ov < s_val[tid] || (ov == s_val[tid] && oi >= 0 && oi < s_idx[tid])) {\n                s_val[tid] = ov;\n                s_idx[tid] = oi;\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n}\n\n// Grow a candidate cluster from seed `seed` inside a single block.\n// maxdist[] is scratch of length N. If record != nullptr, the chosen members\n// are flagged in record[] and also marked in clustered[]. Returns (in\n// s_result) the cardinality of the grown cluster.\n__device__ int growCluster(const double2* __restrict__ points,\n                           const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,\n                           double* __restrict__ maxdist,\n                           const int seed, const int N, const double threshold,\n                           unsigned char* record) {\n    __shared__ double s_val[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ int s_idx[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ int s_next;\n\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    const double INF = std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity();\n\n    // maxdist == INF marks points that can never join this cluster:\n    // already globally clustered, already a member, or over threshold.\n    for (int i = tid; i < N; i += BLOCK_SIZE) {\n        maxdist[i] = (i == seed || clustered[i]) ? the newly added member into maxdist[] and find\n        // the eligible candidate with the smallest resulting diameter.\n        for (int i = tid; i < N; i += BLOCK_SIZE) {\n            double m = maxdist[i];\n            if (m < threshold) {\n                const double2 p = points[i];\n                const double dx = p.x - np.x;\n                const double dy = p.y - np.y;\n                    best_val = m;\n                        best_idx = i;\n                    }\n                } else {\n                    maxdist[i] = INF; // permanently ineligible (max only newpt = s_next;\n        members++;\n    }\n\n    return members;\n}\n\n// Phase 1: every worker block grows candidate clusters for a strided subset\n// of the unclustered seeds and records each   const int* __restrict__ seeds,\n                                const int num_seeds, const int N,\n                                const double threshold,\n                                double*                               const int seed, const int N,\n                               const double threshold,\n                               unsigned char* record) {\n    growCluster(points, const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    if (N == 0) return clusters;\n\n    // Device buffers\n    double2* d_points = CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_points, sizeof(double2) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_clustered, N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_record, N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_seeds, sizeof(int) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_card, sizeof(int) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_points, points.data(), sizeof(double2) * N,\n                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(d_clustered, 0, N));\n\n    // Size the persistent worker pool: enough blocks to saturate the GPU,\n    // capped by available memory for the per-worker maxdist scratch.\n    int device = 0;\n    cudaDeviceProp prop;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDevice(&device));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&prop, device));\n    int workers = prop.multiProcessorCount * 16;\n    size_t free_mem = 0, total_mem = 0;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemGetInfo(&free_mem, &total_mem));\n    const size_t per_worker = sizeof(double) * static_cast<size_t>(N);\n    const size_t mem_cap = static_cast<size_t>(free_mem * 0.9) / per_worker;\n    if (mem_cap < static_cast<size_t>(workers))\n        workers = std::max(1, static_cast<int>(mem_cap));\n    workers = std::min(workers, N);\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_maxdist_pool,\n                          per_worker * static_cast<size_t>(workers)));\n\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\n    std::vector<int> If record != nullptr, the chosen members\n// are flagged in record[] and also marked in clustered[]. Returns (in\n// s_result) the cardinality of the grown cluster.\n__device__ int growCluster(const double2* __restrict__ points,\n                           const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,\n                           double* __restrict__ maxdist,\n                           const int seed, const int N, const double threshold,\n                           unsigned char* record) {\n    __shared__ double s_val[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ int s_idx[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ int s_next;\n\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    const double INF = HUGE_VAL;\n\n    // maxdist == INF marks points that can never join this cluster:\n    // already globally clustered, already a member, or over threshold.\n    for (int i = tid; i < N; i += BLOCK_SIZE) {\n        maxdist[i] = (i == seed || clustered[i]) ? INF : 0.0;\n        if (record) record[i] = (i == seed) ? 1 : 0;\n    }\n    if (record && tid == 0) {\n        const_cast<unsigned char*>(clustered)[seed] = 1;\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    int members = 1;\n    int newpt = seed;\n\n    while (members < N) {\n        const double2 np = points[newpt];\n        double best_val = INF;\n        int best_idx = -1;\n\n        // Fused pass: fold the newly added member into maxdist[] and find\n        // the eligible candidate with the smallest resulting diameter.\n        for (int i = tid; i < N; i += BLOCK_SIZE) {\n            double m = maxdist[i];\n            if (m < threshold) {\n                const double2 p = points[i];\n                const double dx = p.x - np.x;\n                const double dy = p.y - np.y;\n                const double d = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n                if (d > m) m = d;\n                if (m < threshold) {\n                    maxdist[i] = m;\n                    if (m < best_val) {\n                        best_val = m;\n                        best_idx = i;\n                    }\n                } else {\n                    maxdist[i] = INF; // permanently ineligible (max only grows)\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        s_val[tid] = best_val;\n        s_idx[tid] = best_idx;\n        __syncthreads();\n        blockArgmin(s_val, s_idx);\n\n        if (tid == 0) {\n            s_next = s_idx[0];\n            if (s_next >= 0) {\n                maxdist[s_next] = INF;\n                if (record) {\n                    record[s_next] = 1;\n                    const_cast<unsigned char*>(clustered)[s_next] = 1;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        if (s_next < 0) break; A candidate whose running max distance to the members reaches the\n// threshold is dropped permanently (the max only grows). The selection is\n// order-independent: ties on distance are broken by the lowest point index,\n// exactly like the sequential scan, so the atomics-based compaction order\n// does not affect the result.\n//\n// If record != nullptr, chosen members are flagged in record[] and marked\n// in clustered[]. Returns the cardinality of the grown cluster.\n__device__ int growCluster(const double2* __restrict__ points,\n                           const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,\n                           int* __restrict__ cand, double* __restrict__ mdist,\n                           double2* __restrict__ cpos,\n                           const int seed, const int N, const double threshold,\n                           unsigned char* record) {\n    __shared__ double s_val[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ int s_idx[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ int s_next;\n    __shared__ int s_active;\n    __shared__ int s_count;\n\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    const double INF = HUGE_VAL;\n\n    // Build the initial candidate list: every point that is not globally\n    // clustered and not the seed. A pool of persistent\n// worker blocks strides over the seeds; each block grows one candidate\n// cluster at a time, with its threads cooperatively scanning candidates.\n// Each worker maintains an incremental per-candidate array\n//   maxdist[i] = max distance from point i to the current cluster members,\n// so adding a member costs O(N) instead of O(|cluster| * N). Since max is\n// order-independent this is numerically identical to recomputing from\n// scratch, and argmin ties are broken by the lowest point index, exactly\n// matching the sequential algorithm.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                      \\\n    do {                                                                      \\\n        cudaError_t err__ = (call);                                           \\\n        if (err__ != cudaSuccess) {                                           \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d: %s\\n\", #call, __FILE__,  \\\n                    __LINE__, cudaGetErrorString(err__));                     \\\n            exit(1);                                                          \\\n        }                                                                     \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n\n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n\n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n\n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n\n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n\n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// CUDA kernels\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n#define BLOCK_SIZE 256\n\n// Block-wide argmin over (value, index) pairs; ties resolved to the lowest\n// index, matching the sequential scan order. Result left in s_val[0]/s_idx[0].\n__device__ inline void blockArgmin(double* s_val, int* s_idx) {\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    for (int stride = BLOCK_SIZE / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {\n        if (tid < stride) {\n            const double ov = s_val[tid + stride];\n            const int oi = s_idx[tid + stride];\n            if (ov < s_val[tid] || (ov == s_val[tid] && oi >= 0 && oi < s_idx[tid])) {\n                s_val[tid] = ov;\n                s_idx[tid] = oi;\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n}\n\n// Grow a candidate cluster from seed `seed` inside a single block.\n// maxdist[] is scratch of length N. If record != nullptr, the chosen members\n// are flagged in record[] and also marked in clustered[]. Returns (in\n// s_result) the cardinality of the grown cluster.\n__device__ int growCluster(const double2* __restrict__ points,\n                           const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,\n                           double* __restrict__ maxdist,\n                           const int seed, const int N, const double threshold,\n                           unsigned char* record) {\n    __shared__ double s_val[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ int s_idx[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ int s_next;\n\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    const double INF = HUGE_VAL;\n\n    // maxdist == INF marks points that can never join this cluster:\n    // already globally clustered, already a member, or over threshold.\n    for (int i = tid; i < N; i += BLOCK_SIZE) {\n        maxdist[i] = (i == seed || clustered[i]) ? INF : 0.0;\n        if (record) record[i] = (i == seed) ? 1 : 0;\n    }\n    if (record && tid == 0) {\n        const_cast<unsigned char*>(clustered)[seed] = 1;\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    int newpt = s_next;\n        members++;\n    }\n\n    return members;\n}\n\n// Phase 1: every worker block grows candidate clusters for a strided subset\n// of the unclustered seeds and records each < num_seeds; s += gridDim.x) {\n        const int card = growCluster(points, clustered, maxdist, seeds[s], N,\n                                     threshold, nullptr);\n        if (threadIdx.x == 0) cardinality[s] = card;\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n}\n\n// Phase 2: re-grow the winning cluster with a single block, flag its members\n// in `record` and mark them globally                               const int seed, const int N,\n                               const double threshold,\n                               unsigned char* record) {\n    growCluster(points, clustered, maxdist, seed, N, threshold, record);\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm (GPU)\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    if (N == 0) return clusters;\n\n    // Device buffers\n    double2* d_points = nullptr;\n    unsigned char* d_clustered = nullptr;\n    unsigned char* d_record = nullptr;\n    int* d_seeds = nullptr;\n    int* d_card = nullptr;\n    double* d_maxdist_pool = nullptr;\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_points, sizeof(double2) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_clustered, N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_record, N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_seeds, sizeof(int) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_card, sizeof(int) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_points, points.data(), sizeof(double2) * N,\n                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(d_clustered, 0, N));\n\n    // Size the persistent worker pool: enough blocks to saturate the GPU,\n    // capped by available memory for the per-worker maxdist scratch.\n    int device = 0;\n    cudaDeviceProp prop;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDevice(&device));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&prop, device));\n    int workers = prop.multiProcessorCount * 16;\n    size_t static_cast<size_t>(free_mem * 0.9) / per_worker;\n    if (mem_cap < static_cast<size_t>(workers))\n        workers = std::max(1, static_cast<int>(mem_cap));\n    workers = std::min(workers, N);\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_maxdist_pool,\n                          per_worker * static_cast<size_t>(workers)));\n\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\n    std::vector<int>   CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(cardinality.data(), d_card,\n                              sizeof(int) * num_seeds, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n\n        // Pick the seed with the largest candidate cluster; A pool of persistent\n// worker blocks strides over the seeds; each block grows one candidate\n// cluster at a time, with its threads cooperatively scanning candidates.\n// Each worker maintains an incremental per-candidate array\n//   maxdist[i] = max distance from point i to the current cluster members,\n// so adding a member costs O(N) instead of O(|cluster| * N). Since max is\n// order-independent this is numerically identical to recomputing from\n// scratch, and argmin ties are broken by the lowest point index, exactly\n// matching the sequential algorithm.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                      \\\n    do {                                                                      \\\n        cudaError_t err__ = (call);                                           \\\n        if (err__ != cudaSuccess) {                                           \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d: %s\\n\", #call, __FILE__,  \\\n                    __LINE__, cudaGetErrorString(err__));                     \\\n            exit(1);                                                          \\\n        }                                                                     \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n\n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n\n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n\n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n\n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n\n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// CUDA kernels\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n#define BLOCK_SIZE 256\n\n// Block-wide argmin over (value, index) pairs; ties resolved to the lowest\n// index, matching the sequential scan order. Result left in s_val[0]/s_idx[0].\n__device__ inline void blockArgmin(double* s_val, int* s_idx) {\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    for (int stride = BLOCK_SIZE / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {\n        if (tid < stride) {\n            const double ov = s_val[tid + stride];\n            const int oi = s_idx[tid + stride];\n            if (ov < s_val[tid] || (ov == s_val[tid] && oi >= 0 && oi < s_idx[tid])) {\n                s_val[tid] = ov;\n                s_idx[tid] = oi;\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n}\n\n// Grow a candidate cluster from seed `seed` inside a single block.\n//\n// The worker keeps a compacted list of still-eligible candidates in\n// ping-pong scratch buffers (cand: 2N ints, mdist: 2N doubles, cpos:\n// 2N double2), so each growth step only touches candidates that can still\n// join. A candidate whose running max distance to the members reaches the\n// threshold is dropped permanently (the max only grows). The selection is\n// order-independent: ties on distance are broken by the lowest point index,\n// exactly like the sequential scan, so the atomics-based compaction order\n// does not affect the result.\n//\n// If record != nullptr, chosen members are flagged in record[] and marked\n// in clustered[]. Returns the cardinality of the grown cluster.\n__device__ int growCluster(const double2* __restrict__ points,\n                           const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,\n                           int* __restrict__ cand, double* __restrict__ mdist,\n                           double2* __restrict__ cpos,\n                           const int seed, const int N, const double threshold,\n                           unsigned char* record) {\n    __shared__ double s_val[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ int s_idx[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ int s_next;\n    __shared__ int s_active;\n    __shared__ int s_count;\n\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    const double INF = HUGE_VAL;\n\n    // Build the initial candidate list: every point that is not globally\n    // clustered and not the seed. List order is arbitrary.\n    if (tid == 0) {\n        s_count = 0;\n        if (record) const_cast<unsigned char*>(clustered)[seed] = 1;\n    }\n    if (record) {\n        for (int i = tid; i < N; i += BLOCK_SIZE) {\n            record[i] = (i == seed) ? 1 : 0;\n        }\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n    for (int i = tid; i < N; i += BLOCK_SIZE) {\n        if (i != seed && !clustered[i]) {\n            const int k = atomicAdd(&s_count, 1);\n            cand[k] = i;\n            mdist[k] = 0.0;\n            cpos[k] = points[i];\n        }\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    int active = s_count;\n    int cur = 0;\n    int members = 1;\n    int newpt = seed;\n    const double2 seed_pos = points[seed];\n    double2 np = seed_pos;\n\n    while (members < N && active > 0) {\n        if (tid == 0) s_count = 0;\n        double best_val = INF;\n        int best_idx = -1;\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const int* ci = cand + cur * N;\n        const double* mi = mdist + cur * N;\n        const double2* pi = cpos + cur * N;\n        int* co = cand + (cur ^ 1) * N;\n        double* mo = mdist + (cur ^ 1) * N;\n        double2* po = cpos + (cur ^ 1) * N;\n\n        // Fused pass: fold the newly added member into each candidate's\n        // running max distance, drop ineligible candidates, compact the\n        // survivors, and track the best (smallest diameter) candidate.\n        for (int j = tid; j < active; j += BLOCK_SIZE) {\n            const int i = ci[j];\n            if (i == newpt) continue; // just became a member: drop it\n            const double2 p = pi[j];\n            const double dx = p.x - np.x;\n            const double dy = p.y - np.y;\n            const double d = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n            double m = mi[j];\n            if (d > m) m = d;\n            if (m < threshold) {\n                const int k = atomicAdd(&s_count, 1);\n                co[k] = i;\n                mo[k] = m;\n                po[k] = p;\n                if (m < best_val || (m == best_val && i < best_idx)) {\n                    best_val = m;\n                    best_idx = i;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        s_val[tid] = best_val;\n        s_idx[tid] = best_idx;\n        __syncthreads();\n        blockArgmin(s_val, s_idx);\n\n        if (tid == 0) {\n            s_next = s_idx[0];\n            s_active = s_count;\n            if (s_next >= 0 && record) {\n                record[s_next] = 1;\n                const_cast<unsigned char*>(clustered)[s_next] = 1;\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n        const int next = s_next;\n        active = s_active;\n        __syncthreads(); // all reads done before s_count is reset next round\n\n        if (next < 0) break; // no more points can be added\n        newpt = next;\n        np = points[next];\n        members++;\n        cur ^= 1;\n    }\n\n    return members;\n}\n\n// Phase 1: every worker block grows candidate clusters for a strided subset\n// of the unclustered seeds and records each cluster's cardinality.\n__global__ void candidateKernel(const double2* __restrict__ points,\n                                const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,\n                                const int* __restrict__ seeds,\n                                const int num_seeds, const int N,\n                                const double threshold,\n                                double* __restrict__ maxdist_pool,\n                                int* __restrict__ cardinality) {\n    double* maxdist = maxdist_pool + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * N;\n    for (int s = blockIdx.x; s < num_seeds; s += gridDim.x) {\n        const int card = growCluster(points, clustered, maxdist, seeds[s], N,\n                                     threshold, nullptr);\n        if (threadIdx.x == 0) cardinality[s] = card;\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n}\n\n// Phase 2: re-grow the winning cluster with a single block, flag its members\n// in `record` and mark them globally clustered.\n__global__ void growBestKernel(const double2* __restrict__ points,\n                               unsigned char* clustered,\n                               double* __restrict__ maxdist,\n                               const int seed, const int N,\n                               const double threshold,\n                               unsigned char* record) {\n    growCluster(points, clustered, maxdist, seed, N, threshold, record);\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm (GPU)\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    if (N == 0) return clusters;\n\n    // Device buffers\n    double2* d_points = nullptr;\n    unsigned char* d_clustered = nullptr;\n    unsigned char* d_record = nullptr;\n    int* d_seeds = nullptr;\n    int* d_card = nullptr;\n    double* d_maxdist_pool = nullptr;\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_points, sizeof(double2) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_clustered, N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_record, N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_seeds, sizeof(int) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_card, sizeof(int) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_points, points.data(), sizeof(double2) * N,\n                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(d_clustered, 0, N));\n\n    // Size the persistent worker pool: enough blocks to saturate the GPU,\n    // capped by available memory for the per-worker maxdist scratch.\n    int device = 0;\n    cudaDeviceProp prop;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDevice(&device));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&prop, device));\n    int static_cast<size_t>(N);\n    const size_t mem_cap = static_cast<size_t>(free_mem * 0.9) / per_worker;\n    if (mem_cap < static_cast<size_t>(workers))\n        workers = std::max(1, std::vector<unsigned char> record(N);\n\n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        const int num_seeds = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_seeds, unclustered_indices.data(),\n                              sizeof(int) * num_seeds, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n        const int grid = std::min(workers, num_seeds);\n
